Maybe use continuation/optimization. Let's outline a robust solver:

Given n, minimize F(a)=max_k a*a_k subject to sum a=1,a>=0. This is min-max of quadratics. We can use iterative reweighted: Since F = max_k c_k. We can maintain probabilities w_k over lags with sum1, gradient of weighted sum ∑ w_k c_k. If w puts mass on active peaks, minimizing weighted sum and updating w to active set approximates min-max. This is like Frank-Wolfe / mirror descent for convex? But c_k quadratics not convex; weighted sum ∑ w_k ∑_i a_i a_{k-i} = a^T G(a) with G symmetric? Actually ∑ w_k a_i a_{k-i} is a quadratic form with matrix W where W_{i,j}=w_{i+j} (with bounds? For full convolution, w_k for k=i+j). This matrix is Hankel. It may not be positive definite. But if w is distribution over k, matrix can be indefinite. However if w is symmetric? Not guaranteed.

Continuous optimization more complex.

We can use numerical continuous/discrete optimization.

Detailed algorithm plan:

- Use internal function `convolve_fft(a)` to compute full convolution length 2n-1 using rfft with size N=next_fast_len(2n-1). For accuracy, use double, clip tiny negatives. Use normalization sum=1.

- Optimization over p in simplex via softmax parameters z. We can use Adam with dynamic learning rate. Since loss smooth approximation, gradient scale? For sum1, c ~1/n. β ~0.1 max ~0.1/n. w concentrates. Gradient components ~2w*shift p - 2βp. ~O(1/n). Adam step maybe 0.01-0.1. Need adapt.

- To avoid softmax collapse, add regularization ε*(sum a^2) or entropy? We can optimize L + reg * (sum(p^2) - 1/n) maybe to keep positive. Adam implementation top-level:
```
def adam_step(state, grad, lr, b1,b2,eps,t):
  m = b1*m + (1-b1)*grad
  v = b2*v + (1-b2)*grad^2
  mhat=m/(1-b1^t); vhat=v/(1-b2^t)
  param -= lr*mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps)
```
States arrays.

Let's attempt numerical continuous optimization via variational method in code. We can solve for f on grid using projected gradient minimizing smooth max of discrete convolution. - For each, run a few Adam iterations; if time, continue best.

Reduced basis optimization:
Use a positive function of form f_i = exp(θ0 + θ1 sin(ω1 t + φ1)+ θ2 sin(ω2 t+φ2)+ ...). This can represent humps. Number free K=10. Use Nelder-Mead or L-BFGS? We can implement simple random + coordinate? SciPy minimize with Nelder-Mead on 10 dims can explore. Objective compute convolution via FFT O(n log n). n small 64 for reduced. It may find a shape with low score. Since only 10 dims, global multi-start feasible.

But the true optimum may require higher frequency components. We can increase K as needed. Also can use polynomial/Legendre basis to represent monotone shapes: z_i = ∑ θ_k P_k(2t-1). Nonnegative softmax. K=12. Use `scipy.optimize.minimize` method='Nelder-Mead' or 'Powell' for non-differentiable? We have smooth differentiable; L-BFGS-B on z coefficients (with constant omitted) can work. But softmax of basis may create local minima. Multi-start random θ.

Alternatively, optimize coefficients of a positive polynomial amplitude: f_i = (c0 + c1 t + c2 t^2 + ... + cK t^K)^2 + ε? This ensures nonnegative and analytic. Sum normalize. The square of polynomial can create humps. But square of polynomial has even nature; if polynomial has sign changes, f has zeros. Could be powerful. Optimize coefficients with L-BFGS (dimension K+1 ~ 7) using smooth loss. Nonnegative automatic. This may find shapes with interior zeros. Let's use basis: raw R(t)=∑_{k=0}^K θ_k t^k; f_i = R(t_i)^2 + 1e-6. Normalize. This is positive. K=6 or8. Objective differentiable. Use multi-start. The squared polynomial can model monotone increasing (R linear positive), humps (R changes sign -> zeros). Good.

But final discrete a may have many small values; fine.

We can also use trigonometric positive form: f_i = (1 + sum amplitudes cos(2π m t + phase))_+? Positivity hard. Squared trig polynomial: f_i = (∑ θ_k cos(2π k t)) /??? maybe can be negative; square of trig sum nonnegative. f_i = (Σ_{m=0}^K θ_m cos(2π m t + φ_m))^2 + ε. This can make oscillatory positive. Dimension ~2K. Use L-BFGS-B.

However maximizing smooth max of convolution might favor shapes with high values at some points; squared polynomial amplitude can go large, but normalize.

Maybe simpler: Since n can be chosen, we can use random search over K-parameter shapes and select best. The final can then full refine.

```
def gradient_from_weights(w, p):
    n=len(p)
    N=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    wr = w[:2*n-1]
    pref = np.fft.rfft(wr, N)
    p_rev = p[::-1]
    conv = np.fft.irfft(pref * np.fft.rfft(p_rev, N), N)
    # conv[m]=∑_j w_j p_rev[m-j]? We derived g_i = conv[i+n-1] for conv=w * p_rev. Let's verify with small manual.
```
Let's re-verify derived formula carefully to avoid wrong gradient.
We need g_i = ∑_j w_{i+j} p_j for i=0..n-1.
Let r = p[::-1], length n: r[t]=p[n-1-t]. Linear convolution h = w * r: h[m]=∑_{t=0}^{n-1} r[t] w[m-t] (with w index 0..2n-2). =∑_{t} p[n-1-t] w[m-t]. Let j=n-1-t => t=n-1-j. h[m]=∑_j p[j] w[m-(n-1-j)] =∑_j p[j] w[m-n+1+j]. We want i= m-n+1, so m=i+n-1. Then h[i+n-1]=∑_j p[j] w[i+j] = g_i. Good. So g = h[n-1 : 2n-1] (indices n-1 to 2n-2 inclusive length n). Good.
Then gradient wrt p is 2*g. Gradient wrt z (softmax) = p*(gp - p*dot(gp,p)), but for L, dot(gp,p)=2∑w c? Let's just compute dot numerically: `dz = p * (gp - np.dot(p, gp))`. Since F = β log∑exp(c/β), ∂F/∂p = gp (where gp_i=2∑w_{i+j}p_j). Then softmax gradient = p*(gp - dot(p,gp)). This is robust. Add optional reg.

Let's define scoring internal:
```
def internal_score(a):
    n=len(a)
    if n==0: return inf
    s=sum(a)
    if s<=0: return inf
    conv=full_convolve_fft(a)
    m=np.max(conv)
    return 2.0*n*m/(s*s)
```
Since a normalized sum1, score=2n max.


Projection onto simplex function:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    n=len(v)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=0; cum=0; rho=0; found=False
    for i in range(n):
       cum += u[i]
       css = cum - (i+1)
       tau = css/(i+1)
       if u[i] - tau > 0:
           found=True; break
    theta=tau
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
Need ensure sum1. Standard.

Full local refinement function using projected gradient:
```
def refine_p(p, beta0, time_limit, min_iter=100, max_iter=20000, initial_score=None):
   p = project_simplex(p)
   n=len(p)
   best_local=p.copy(); best_loss=internal_score(p) # exact max maybe
   beta = max(1e-6, beta0 * max(p*p))
   step=1.0
   stagn=0
   last_imp=inf
   iter=0
   while time < limit:
      c=full_convolve_fft(p)
      L, w = smooth_max(c,beta)
      gp=2*corr(w,p)
      # optional center gradient? no
      z = p - step*gp
      p_new = project_simplex(z)
      c_new=full_convolve_fft(p_new)
      L_new, w_new = smooth_max(c_new,beta)
      # Accept if L_new < L or true score improved? Use L.
      if finite and L_new <= L + 1e-14:
          p=p_new
          # true score maybe
          score=2*n*np.max(c_new)
          if score < best_score - tol:
              best_local=p.copy(); best_score=score; stagn=0
          else stagn+=1
          step=min(1.0, step*1.02)
      else:
          step*=0.5
          if step < 0.01:
             # perturb
             p = project(p + 0.01*rng.normal(size=n))
             step=0.1
          stagn+=1
      iter+=1
      # beta decay if many iters without true improvement
      if iter % 100 ==0:
          beta *= 0.8
          if beta < max(1e-8, current_max/50): beta=...
      # if stagn large: perturb and reset
```

Let's write code skeleton:
```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import differential_evolution

def next_fast_len(n):
   # power of two >= n
   k=1
   while k<n: k*=2
   return k
```
Could use `scipy.fft.next_fast_len` but not sure import. Use power of two for simplicity.

`full_convolve_fft(a)`:
```
def full_convolve_fft(a):
    n=len(a)
    if n<4: return np.convolve(a,a)
    N=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    f=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(f*f,N)[:2*n-1]
    # remove tiny negatives
    if c.min() < 0 and c.min() > -1e-12: c += -c.min()
    return c
```
For a with sum1, values ~1e-6; c ~1/n. Use direct for n small to avoid FFT error:
```
if n <= 128: return np.convolve(a,a)
```
Direct C is fast and exact-ish. For optimization many calls n=128 direct 16k mult, 10k calls=160M maybe ok? FFT maybe faster. Use FFT for all >64.

`soft_mx(c,beta)`:
```
def soft_mx(c,beta):
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if beta <=0: 
       w=np.zeros_like(c); w[c==m]=1/np.sum(c==m); return m,w
    x=(c-m)/beta
    ex=np.exp(x)
    s=float(np.sum(ex))
    L=m+beta*float(np.log(s))
    w=ex/s
    return L,w
```
Since x<=0, safe.

`corr_weights(w,p)`:
```
def corr_weights(w,p):
    n=len(p); L=2*n-1
    # ensure w length L
    N=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    wr=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
    pr=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],N)
    h=np.fft.irfft(wr*pr,N)
    return h[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Test orientation again with n=2 manual: w=[w0,w1,w2], p=[p0,p1]. h index1 = w0p0+w1p1? Let's compute h via formula h[m]=∑_j p_j w[m-n+1+j], n=2: m=1: j0 w0p0, j1 w1p1. Desired g0=w0p0+w1p1. OK. m=2: w1p0+w2p1 desired g1. Great.
Then `gp = 2.0*corr_weights(w,p)`.

`project_simplex(v)` robust:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=len(v)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.arange(1,n+1)
    cond = u > (cssv - 1.0)/rho
    # find last True? Standard returns max(1 - (cssv[k]-1)/(k+1)) for max k satisfying u[k] > (sum u[:k+1]-1)/(k+1)
    idx = np.where(cond)[0]
    if len(idx)==0:
       theta=float(np.sum(v)-1)/len(v) ??
    k=idx[-1]
    theta=(cssv[k]-1.0)/(k+1)
    p=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    p /= p.sum() if p.sum()>0 else 1
    return p
```
Need condition u_i > (sum_{j<=i} u_j -1)/ (i+1). Use zero-based indices; rho = np.arange(1,n+1); cond = u > (cssv -1)/rho. For uniform v, cond all True? u=1/n, cssv i+1=1? (cssv-1)/rho=0, u>0 yes all; k=n-1, theta=(1-1)/n=0. Good.

`normalize_nonneg(a, floor=0.0)`:
```
a=np.maximum(np.asarray(a,float),0)
s=a.sum()
if s<=0: return None
a = a/s
# optional floor? not here, floor would alter; just positive small maybe
return a
```

`internal_score(a)` using exact or FFT:
```
def internal_score(a):
   n=len(a); s=sum(a)
   if s<=1e-15: return inf
   conv=full_convolve_fft(a)
   m=np.max(conv)
   return float(2.0*n*m/(s*s))
```
For exact final use `npc.convolve`.

`true_score(a)`:
```
def true_score(a):
   n=len(a); s=a.sum()
   if s<1e-9: return inf
   m=np.max(np.convolve(a,a))
   return 2*n*m/(s*s)
```

`update_best(best, score)`: since no closures, pass list/array and return.

```
a=1.0+eta*(t-0.5)
a=np.maximum(a,1e-12); a/=sum
```

Local refinement function (projected smooth):
```
def refine_p(p, budget, beta_frac=0.1, max_iter=20000, noise_scale=0.02):
```
Use time_limit absolute. It returns best p.
Implementation details:
- Input p normalized.
- `best_p=p.copy(); best_score=internal_score(p)`.
- `c=full_convolve_fft(p); beta=max(1e-7, beta_frac*max(c))`.
- `step=1.0`; `no_imp=0`; `it=0`.
- Loop while time < deadline:
   - if it % 200 ==0: beta *= 0.75; if beta < 1e-9*maxc: beta=1e-9*maxc.
   - c=full_convolve_fft(p); L,w=soft_mx(c,beta)
   - gp=2*corr_weights(w,p)
   - # optional: if beta small, replace weights with active uniform? Maybe skip.
   - Try step with projection:
       z=p-step*gp
       p2=project_simplex(z)
       c2=full_convolve_fft(p2); L2,_=soft_mx(c2,beta)
       score2=2*n*np.max(c2)
       if finite and (L2 < L - 1e-15 or score2 < best_score - 1e-12):
           p=p2
           if score2 < best_score:
               best_score=score2; best_p=p.copy(); no_imp=0
           else no_imp +=1
           step=min(1.0, step*1.05)
       else:
           step*=0.5
           if step < 0.02:
              # perturb
              p = project_simplex(p + step*full_convolve_fft? no random)
              p = project_simplex(p + 0.02*rng.normal(size=n))
              best? score
              step=0.5
              no_imp+=20
   - if no_imp > 2000:
       # larger perturbation or restart beta
       p = project_simplex(p + 0.05*rng.normal(size=n))
       # ensure finite; maybe mix with best
       if rng.random()<0.5: p=0.7*best_p+0.3*random_p; project
       no_imp=0; step=0.5; beta=max(beta,0.2*maxc)
   - it +=1
```
But computing c2 every proj requires second FFT. Okay.
```

def theta_grad(theta,basis,n,beta,reg):
   p=profile
   c=conv; L,w=soft
   gp=2*corr
   gz = p*(gp - np.dot(p,gp))
   if reg !=0: gz += 2*reg*n*p  # wait previous gradient wrt p for reg = 2*reg*n? Let's derive reg term R=γ*(n*sum p^2 -1). dR/dp_i = 2γ n p_i. Then gz = (gp +2γ n p) * p? No softmax chain: dR/dz = (dR/dp) dot dp/dz. dR/dp = 2γ n p. Then grad_z = p∘dR/dp - p*(p·dR/dp) = p*(2γ n p) - p*(2γ n sum p^2) = 2γ n p (p - S2). Not simply add 2γ n p to gz from L? For L we had gz = p*(gp - Sgp) where Sgp=∑p gp. For R, dR/dp vector r; grad_z = p*r - p*(p·r). We can compute concisely:
       vp = gp  (dL/dp)
       vr = 2*reg*n*p
       total_vp = vp + vr
       gz = p*(total_vp - np.dot(p,total_vp))
   gtheta = basis.T @ gz
```
This is correct.
```

```
Let's specify `theta_eval(theta,basis,beta,reg)`:
```
def theta_eval(theta,basis,beta,reg):
    n=basis.shape[0]
    z=basis @ theta
    z = np.clip(z, -50, 50)
    z -= np.max(z)
    p=np.exp(z)+1e-12
    p = p / p.sum()
    c=full_convolve_fft(p)
    L,w = soft_mx(c,beta)
    gp=2.0*corr_weights(w,p)
    total_vp = gp
    if reg !=0:
        total_vp = total_vp + (2.0*reg*float(n))*p
    dot = float(np.dot(p,total_vp))
    gz = p*(total_vp - dot)
    gtheta = basis.T @ gz
    loss = 2.0*float(n)*L + reg*(float(n)*float(np.sum(p*p)) -1.0)
    score = 2.0*float(n)*float(np.max(c))
    return loss, score, gtheta, p
```
Note: The derivative of `loss = 2n L + reg(...)` is `2n gp + 2reg n p`. We included reg dR/dp=2reg n p, and factor `2n` outside: Actually if loss = 2n*L + R, where R=γ(n S2 -1), derivative wrt p: 2n*gp + 2γ n p. In `theta_eval`, gp already gradient wrt p for L (not scaled). We should total_vp = 2n*gp + 2γ n p. But multiplying entire gradient by positive scalar (2n) doesn't affect Adam much; for lr scaling, use `grad_theta = 2n*(gp + γ p) - p*(...)`? Let's compute cleanly:
Let f_p = 2n*gp + 2γ n p. Then soft-Z gradient = p*f_p - p*(p·f_p). Since factor 2n common with γ? If we use `vp = gp + γ p`, then gradient = 2n*(p*vp - p*(p·vp)). Because p·(2nvp)=2n p·vp. Constant 2n can be absorbed into lr. For simplicity, ignore scaling by 2n and set `vp = gp + γ p`, gtheta = basis.T @ (p*(vp - dot)). This is okay; lr adjusted. Loss reported should be true score, not used in gradient? Adam uses gradient only. We'll use `score` for best.
```
vp = gp
if reg: vp = vp + reg*p  # since dR/dp=2γ n p, relative to gp scale? gp already from L (derivative of L). Actually L derivative gp; score=2nL derivative 2n gp. R derivative 2γn p. If set vp=gp + γ p and ignore 2n factor, direction same if γ corresponds. Fine.
gz = p*(vp - dot)
gtheta = basis.T @ gz
```
Use reg maybe 1e-4 to 1e-3. The gp magnitude O(1), reg*p O(reg). OK.
Loss for monitoring could be `score` (true). But Adam gradient approximates smooth L. Good.

`theta_adam` implementation:
```
def theta_adam(theta0,basis,n_iters,beta,reg,lr,time_deadline, rng, verbose=False):
    theta=theta0.copy().astype(float)
    m=np.zeros_like(theta); v=np.zeros_like(theta); t=0
    b1=0.9; b2=0.99; eps=1e-8
    # initial eval to set beta? caller passes.
    best_p=None; best_score=inf
    while t<n_iters and time.monotonic()<deadline:
       loss,score,gtheta,p=theta_eval(theta,basis,beta,reg)
       if np.isfinite(score) and score<best_score:
           best_score=score; best_p=p.copy()
       if not np.isfinite(gtheta): lr*=0.5; continue
       t+=1
       m=b1*m+(1-b1)*gtheta
       v=b2*v+(1-b2)*gtheta*gtheta
       mh=m/(1-b1**t); vh=v/(1-b2**t)
       theta = theta - lr*mh/(np.sqrt(vh)+eps)
       theta = np.clip(theta, -6, 6)
       # occasionally reduce lr if loss score increases? Optionally track smooth_loss
       if t%100==0:
           # maybe update beta based on current best? not
           pass
    # final eval
    if best_p is None: ...
    return theta, best_p, best_score
```
Need `n` in theta_eval from basis shape; no separate.

```

   current max =...

   best_theta, p, score = theta_adam(best_theta, basis, 300, beta, reg, lr, deadline,...)

```
`logit_eval` similar to theta_eval but no basis. We'll create a generic `softmax_eval(z,beta,reg)` returning.

Use this for both θ? Could create basis transform in θ_eval by z=basis@theta. So one eval function.

```
def softmax_eval(z,beta,reg):
   p=softmax(z)
   c=full_convolve_fft(p)
   L,w=soft_mx(c,beta)
   gp=2*corr_weights(w,p)
   vp=gp + reg*p
   dot=np.dot(p,vp)
   gz=p*(vp-dot)
   score=2*len(p)*np.max(c)
   return L,score,gz,p
```
For θ gradient: `gz = softmax_eval(basis@theta,...).gz; gtheta=basis.T@gz`.
```

def logit_anneal(p, deadline, rng, base_lr=0.05, reg=1e-4):
   n=len(p); z=np.log(p+1e-12); z-=mean
   best_p=p.copy(); best_score=internal_score(p)
   c=full_convolve_fft(p); beta=max(1e-7,0.2*max(c))
   m,v zeros; t=0; last_imp=0; lr=base_lr
   while time<deadline:
      c=full_convolve_fft(softmax(z)); L,w=soft_mx(c,beta); score=2n*max(c)
      gp=2corr; vp=gp+reg*p; dot; gz
      # center gradient and z
      gz -= gz.mean(); z -= z.mean()
      t++; adam update; clip z [-50,50]
      if score < best_score -1e-9: best...; last_imp=0
      else last_imp++
      if t%100==0:
         beta = max(1e-8, 0.05*max(c)) # sharp
      if last_imp > 2000:
         # perturb z
         z = z + 0.1*rng.normal(size=n); z-=mean
         beta = max(beta,0.2*max(c))
         last_imp=0
   return best_p,best_score,softmax(z)

def logit_anneal(p0, deadline, rng, lr=0.1, reg=1e-4, min_iter=200):
    n=len(p0); z=np.log(p0+1e-12); z-=np.mean(z)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); b1=0.9;b2=0.999;eps=1e-8)t=0
    p=softmax(z)
    c=full_convolve_fft(p); maxc=c.max(); beta=max(1e-12,0.2*maxc)
    best_score=2*n*maxc; best_p=p.copy(); best_z=z.copy()
    stamp=time.monotonic(); t=0
    while time<deadline:
       t+=1
       # compute L,w, gp
       mval=c.max(); x=(c-mval)/beta; ex=np.exp(x); s=sum(ex); L=mval+beta*log(s); w=ex/s
       gp=2*corr_weights(w,p)
       vp=gp + reg*p
       gz = p*(vp - np.dot(p,vp))
       gz -=.gz.mean()
       m=...; v=...
       z = z - lr*...
       z = np.clip(z,-50,50); z -= z.mean()
       p=softmax(z)
       c=full_convolve_fft(p); maxc=c.max(); score=2*n*maxc
       if score < best_score:
          best_score=score; best_p=p.copy(); best_z=z.copy(); no_imp=0
       else: no_imp +=1
       if t % 200 ==0:
          beta = max(1e-12, beta*0.7)
       if no_imp > 2000:
          z = 0.8*best_z + 0.2*rng.normal(size=n)
          z=np.clip(z,-50,50); z-=z.mean()
          p=softmax(z); c=full_convolve_fft(p); maxc=c.max(); beta=max(1e-8,0.2*maxc)
          no_imp=0
           # maybe reset Adam states
          m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t_adam=0? Need t_adam separate.
```

Implementation details for logit_anneal:
```
def logit_anneal(p0, deadline, rng=None, lr=0.1, reg=1e-4):
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(123)
    p = np.asarray(p0,float).copy()
    p=np.maximum(p,1e-12); s=p.sum(); p/=s
    n=len(p)
    z=np.log(p); z-=np.mean(z)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); adam_t=0; b1=0.9;b2=0.999;eps=1e-8
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=internal_score(p)
    c=full_convolve_fft(p); maxc=float(c.max()); beta=max(1e-12,0.25*maxc); no_imp=0
    base_lr=lr
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        # if beta too small, active weights
        mval=float(np.max(c))
        if beta <= 1e-12:
            w=np.zeros_like(c)
            mask=c >= mval - 1e-12
            if not np.any(mask): mask=np.zeros_like(c); mask[mval==mval]? better idx=np.argmax(c); mask[idx]=True
            w[mask]=1.0/np.count_nonzero(mask)
            L=mval
        else:
            x=(c-mval)/beta
            ex=np.exp(x)
            sEx=float(np.sum(ex))
            if sEx <=0 or not finite: ex=np.zeros_like(c); idx=np.argmax(c); ex[idx]=1; sEx=1
            w=ex/sEx
            L=mval + beta*float(np.log(sEx))
        gp=2.0*corr_weights(w,p)
        vp=gp + reg*p
        dot=float(np.dot(p,vp))
        gz=p*(vp-dot)
        gz -= float(np.mean(gz))
        adam_t +=1
        m = b1*m + (1-b1)*gz
        v = b2*v + (1-b2)*(gz*gz)
        mh=m/(1-b1**adam_t)
        vh=v/(1-b2**adam_t)
        z = z - lr*mh/(np.sqrt(vh)+eps)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
            z = best_z.copy() if best_z else z
            m.fill(0); v.fill(0); adam_t=0
            continue
        z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
        z -= float(np.mean(z))
        p = softmax_from_z(z)  # top-level
        c = full_convolve_fft(p)
        maxc=float(np.max(c))
        score=2.0*float(n)*maxc
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score=score; best_p=p.copy(); best_z=z.copy(); no_imp=0
            # occasionally recenter beta? 
        else:
            no_imp +=1
            if no_imp % 500 ==0:
                lr = max(0.02, lr*0.98)
        if adam_t % 200 ==0:
            beta = max(1e-12, beta*0.7)
        if no_imp > max(500, 10*n):
            # restart with best plus noise
            z = 0.85*best_z + 0.3*rng.normal(size=n)
            z=np.clip(z,-50,50); z-=mean
            p=softmax(z); c=full_convolve_fft(p); maxc=c.max(); beta=max(1e-10,0.25*maxc)
            m.fill(0); v.fill(0); adam_t=0; lr=base_lr; no_imp=0
    return best_p,best_score
```
Need `best_z` initialized `z`.
```

def make_profiles_initial(n, rng, count):
   profiles=[]
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n); u=2*t-1
   for eta in [1.0,1.2,1.4,1.6,1.8,2.0]:
      a=1.0+eta*(t-0.5); a=np.maximum(a,1e-12); a/=sum; profiles.append(a)
   for a_,b_ in [(1.5,-1),(1.8,-2),(1.6,-1),(1.2,-0.5),(1.0,1),(2.0,-2)]:
      val=1+a_*u+b_*u**3; val=np.maximum(val,1e-12); profiles.append(val/sum)
   # random smooth
   basis=make_basis_mixed(n,13)

   basisp=make_basis_poly(n,12)

   # random blocky maybe

      p=(rng.random(n)>0.5).astype(float)+0.05; p/=sum; profiles.append(p)
   return profiles

```
def param_adam(theta0, basis, deadline, rng, lr=0.2, reg=1e-4):
   n=basis.shape[0]

   best_theta=theta.copy(); best_p=p.copy(); best_score=2*n*maxc
   while time<deadline:
      # eval with beta
      ... compute c,L,w,gp ...
      vp=gp+reg*p; dot; gz=p*(vp-dot); gtheta=basis.T@gz
      # scale gtheta? Since basis values O1, okay.

      # check finite, beta update, no_imp perturb
```

Potential edge cases:
- `full_convolve_fft` for n=1? Our n>=32. Good.
- `corr_weights` length of w should be 2n-1. In `smooth_conv_grad`, w from c length 2n-1.
- FFT power-of-two next_fast_len: For n=128, 2n-1=255 -> N=256. For n=129, N=512. Good.
- In `full_convolve_fft`, using `N=next_power_of_two(2*n-1)`; `np.fft.irfft(f*f,N)[:2*n-1]`. For real input, f*f real? rfft of real gives complex, product, irfft fine. For large n, small negative; clip.

Regularization reg in grad: In `smooth_conv_grad`, gp is derivative of L (not score). If we add reg to vp, okay. But in score tracking, no reg.

`param_adam` gradient scaling: Since loss score=2nL, derivative wrt θ=2n basis.T gz. We ignore 2n factor; lr absorb. Reg derivative similarly; okay.

Beta in param_adam: initial maxc from p; after update, maxc from c. Decrease beta every 200 by factor0.75. When no improvement, reset beta to 0.25*maxc and theta perturbation.

`logit_anneal` begins with log(p) but p may have zeros (from interpolation/deterministic). Use min 1e-12. If p from profile softmax positive. Good.

Time slicing: Use `time.monotonic()`.
In propose, deadlines should subtract 10s safety.

Detailed functions:
```
def tuple_first(item):
    return item[0]
```

`next_pow2`:
```
def next_pow2(n):
    k=1
    while k<n:
       k <<=1
    return max(2,k)
```

`full_convolve_fft`:
```
def full_convolve_fft(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n <= 64:
       return np.convolve(a,a)
    N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2*n-1]
    minc=float(c.min())
    if minc < 0.0:
       if minc > -1e-9:
          c = c - minc
       else:
          c = np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```
For n<=64 direct, many calls in param_adam n=128 uses FFT. Good.

`soft_mx` as above; if beta <=1e-14 active.

`corr_weights`:
```
def corr_weights(w,p):
    n=len(p); N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],N)
    h=np.fft.irfft(fw*fp,N)
    return h[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Ensure w length 2n-1.

`smooth_conv_grad`:
```
def smooth_conv_grad(p,beta):
    c=full_convolve_fft(p)
    L,w=soft_mx(c,beta)
    gp=2.0*corr_weights(w,p)
    return c,L,gp
```

`softmax_from_z`: add tiny to avoid zero.
```
def softmax_from_z(z):
   z=np.asarray(z,float).ravel()
   z=np.clip(z,-50,50)
   z=z-np.max(z)
   e=np.exp(z)
   p=e+1e-12
   s=p.sum()
   if s<=0: p=np.ones_like(e)/len(e)
   return p/s
```

`profile_from_basis(theta,basis)`.

`make_basis_mixed(n,K)`:
```
def make_basis_mixed(n,K):
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    u=2.0*t-1.0
    cols=[u]
    m=1
    while len(cols)<K:
       ang=2*np.pi*m*t
       cols.append(np.cos(ang)); cols.append(np.sin(ang))
       m+=1
       if len(cols)>K: break
    return np.column_stack(cols[:K])
```
If K=1, just u. Use K at least 3.

`make_basis_poly(n,K)`:
```
def make_basis_poly(n,K):
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n); u=2*t-1
    cols=[]
    for k in range(1,K+1): cols.append(u**k)
    return np.column_stack(cols)
```
Powers up to11 okay.

`make_linear_profile(n,eta)`, `make_cubic_profile(n,a,b)`.

`make_deterministic_profiles(n, rng_count?)` returns list.

`make_random_smooth_profiles(n, count, rng)`:
```
def make_random_smooth_profiles(n,count,rng):
   profiles=[]
   profiles.extend(make_deterministic_profiles(n))
   bm=make_basis_mixed(n,13)
   bp=make_basis_poly(n,12)
   for i in range(count):
       th=rng.uniform(-3,3,bm.shape[1]); profiles.append(profile_from_basis(th,bm))
       th=rng.uniform(-3,3,bp.shape[1]); profiles.append(profile_from_basis(th,bp))
   # random binary smooth? maybe block
   for i in range(max(1,count//4)):
       # random low frequency via basis and round? use smooth random Fourier
       th=rng.uniform(-2,2,bm.shape[1]); p=profile_from_basis(th,bm)
       # threshold-ish: p = p**3 / sum
       q=np.power(p,3); q/=q.sum(); profiles.append(q)
   return profiles
```
Deterministic profiles include linear/cubic. Good.

`interpolate_profile` as above.

`param_adam`:
```
def param_adam(theta0,basis,deadline,rng=None,lr=0.2,reg=1e-4):
   if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
   theta=np.asarray(theta0,float).copy()
   n=basis.shape[0]
   m=np.zeros_like(theta); v=np.zeros_like(theta); adam_t=0; b1=0.9;b2=0.999;eps=1e-8
   p=profile_from_basis(theta,basis)
   c=full_convolve_fft(p); maxc=float(c.max()); beta=max(1e-12,0.25*maxc)
   best_theta=theta.copy(); best_p=p.copy(); best_score=2.0*float(n)*maxc
   no_imp=0; base_lr=lr
   while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       # compute with current p/c/beta? We can avoid recompute p if no theta change? Initial then loop starts by eval; after beta update c current.
       L,w=soft_mx(c,beta)
       gp=2.0*corr_weights(w,p)
       vp=gp+reg*p
       dot=float(np.dot(p,vp))
       gz=p*(vp-dot)
       gtheta=basis.T@gz
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(gtheta)):
          theta=best_theta.copy(); m.fill(0); v.fill(0); adam_t=0; continue
       adam_t +=1
       m=b1*m+(1-b1)*gtheta
       v=b2*v+(1-b2)*(gtheta*gtheta)
       mh=m/(1-b1**adam_t); vh=v/(1-b2**adam_t)
       theta=theta-lr*mh/(sqrt(vh)+eps)
       if not finite theta: reset
       theta=np.clip(theta,-6,6)
       p=profile_from_basis(theta,basis)
       c=full_convolve_fft(p); maxc=c.max(); score=2n*maxc
       if score < best_score:
          update; no_imp=0
       else no_imp +=1
       if adam_t%200==0: beta=max(1e-12,beta*0.75)
       if no_imp>max(300,5*theta.shape[0]):
          theta=best_theta + 0.5*rng.normal(size=theta.shape)
          theta=clip
          p=profile; c=...; beta=max(1e-10,0.25*maxc); m.fill(0); v.fill(0); adam_t=0; lr=base_lr; no_imp=0
   return best_theta,best_p,best_score
```
One issue: `gtheta = basis.T@gz` uses gz gradient wrt z. If basis has columns with varying scales, okay. But in `vp=gp+reg*p`, gp gradient of L; for score derivative should multiply by2n; gtheta scale small; lr initial 0.2 maybe okay. If gradient too small, no movement? Adam normalizes magnitude, so lr in theta units. Good.

`logit_anneal` same but states length n. Use `smooth_conv_grad(p,beta)` to compute c,L? But soft_mx recomputed. We'll implement like param.

```
def add_candidate(candidates,p,score,limit):
    if score is None or not finite: return
    if len(candidates)<limit:
       candidates.append((score,p.copy()))
       candidates.sort(key=tuple_first)
    else:
       max_idx=0; max_val=candidates[0][0]
       for i in range(1,len(candidates)):
          if candidates[i][0]>max_val: max_idx=i; max_val=candidates[i][0]
       if score < max_val:
          candidates[max_idx]=(score,p.copy()); candidates.sort(key=tuple_first)
```
Sorting list of (score,array) with key tuple_first compares scores only; if equal no problem. `np.array` not compared because key returns float.

One more idea: To beat target, perhaps need sequences with zeros? Logit softmax keeps positive, but zeros might be useful? Zeros increase n penalty, likely not. If optimum has zeros, we can at end set values below threshold to 0 and normalize, but that changes n and may worsen. Don't.

Potential problem with logit softmax: It can't represent exact very low values below exp(-50)/sum ~1e-22; if optimum requires zeros, approximate. OK.

Let's adjust learning rates:
- param_adam lr=0.3 maybe; θ dim small. Use 0.3.
- logit_anneal lr=0.08; n dim large. Use 0.08.
Add reg=1e-5 maybe. Too much reg biases to uniform and hurts (uniform score2). Use reg=1e-6. The gp magnitude maybe O(1), reg small.

Beta initial: 0.25 max. Decrease every 150? Use 100 for faster sharpening. In logit, if 100* beta*0.7 -> after 1000 iters 0.25*0.7^10=0.006. Good. Use every 150 factor0.8? Let's set every 100 factor0.75.

No_imp patience: param dim*2+200. logit 500+ n.

Time per task: We must avoid exceeding deadline by not starting new task if less than min. In loops, check `if time.monotonic() > phase_deadline: break`.

At the end, if no improvement below previous? It prints.

Let's now write final code. Ensure imports only numpy, time. No scipy to reduce. We don't use cvxpy. Good.

One concern: Direct `np.convolve` in exact for n=3000 maybe okay but if many candidates maybe slow. Limit exact to top 6 candidates and size<=2000. Use threshold 2048.

Another concern: `full_convolve_fft` for n=128 and many calls uses `N=256`; For c = irfft(fa*fa,N), the output length N, high frequencies due zero padding; fine. For real input, product `fa*fa` (not conjugate) yields complex; irfft OK.

Gradient orientation test again with n=3 quickly: p=[p0,p1,p2], w length5. Desired g_i=∑_j w_{i+j}p_j.
r=p[::-1]=[p2,p1,p0]. h=w*r.
h[m]=∑_{j=0}^{2} p_j w[m-3+1+j]? Formula h[i+n-1] with n=3: h[2]=j0 w0p0+j1 w1p1+j2 w2p2 => g0=w0p0+w1p1+w2p2 (i=0: w0,w1,w2). correct. h[3]=w1p0+w2p1+w3p2 => g1 (i=1: w1,w2,w3). h[4]=w2p0+w3p1+w4p2 => g2. good.

`sv` in project_simplex? Not used. Remove to avoid? Could keep but not needed. We can omit project_simplex entirely since not used. But interpolation/deterministic direct simplex. No need. We'll not include project_simplex to save.

`logit_anneal` uses `smooth_conv_grad`? We can implement using `full_convolve_fft` + `soft_mx` + `corr_weights` to avoid function call overhead? Function call okay. We'll use direct in loop.

Let's code.

After code, final block only. Ensure no lambda.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def tuple_first(item):
    """Sort key for (score, array) candidates."""
    return item[0]


def next_pow2(n):
    """Smallest power of two >= n, at least 2."""
    k = 1
    while k < n:
        k <<= 1
    return max(2, k)


def full_convolve_fft(a):
    """Full linear convolution of a real 1D array with itself."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([])
    if n <= 64:
        return np.convolve(a, a)
    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:2 * n - 1]
    mn = float(c.min())
    if mn < 0.0:
        if mn > -1e-9:
            c = c - mn
        else:
            c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def soft_mx(c, beta):
    """Smooth max of c with width beta, returning value and interpolating weights."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0, np.array([])
    mval = float(c.max())
    if beta <= 1e-14:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        mask = c >= mval - 1e-12
        if not np.any(mask):
            idx = int(np.argmax(c))
            mask[idx] = True
        w[mask] = 1.0 / float(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        return mval, w
    x = (c - mval) / beta
    ex = np.exp(x)
    s = float(np.sum(ex))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        ex = np.zeros_like(c)
        ex[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        s = 1.0
    val = mval + beta * float(np.log(s))
    w = ex / s
    return val, w


def corr_weights(w, p):
    """Compute h[i] = sum_j w[i+j] p[j], for i=0..len(p)-1."""
    n = len(p)
    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
    h = np.fft.irfft(fw * fp, N)
    return h[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def softmax_from_z(z):
    """Numerically stable softmax with a small positive floor."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    p = e + 1e-12
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones_like(e) / float(e.size)
    else:
        p = p / s
    return p


def normalize_nonneg(a):
    """Clip to nonnegative and normalize to sum 1, or None if invalid."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-30:
        return None
    return a / s


def internal_score(a):
    """Fast score using FFT convolution."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-30:
        return np.inf
    c = full_convolve_fft(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    mval = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mval):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * mval / (s * s))


def true_score(a):
    """Exact score using numpy direct convolution."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-30:
        return np.inf
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    mval = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mval):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * mval / (s * s))


def add_candidate(candidates, p, score, limit=16):
    """Maintain top-score candidate list without lambdas/closures."""
    if p is None or score is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if len(candidates) < limit:
        candidates.append((float(score), p))
        candidates.sort(key=tuple_first)
    else:
        worst_idx = 0
        worst_val = candidates[0][0]
        for i in range(1, len(candidates)):
            if candidates[i][0] > worst_val:
                worst_val = candidates[i][0]
                worst_idx = i
        if float(score) < worst_val:
            candidates[worst_idx] = (float(score), p)
            candidates.sort(key=tuple_first)


def make_basis_mixed(n, K):
    """Mix linear drift and trigonometric harmonics; no constant column."""
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    u = 2.0 * t - 1.0
    cols = [u]
    m = 1
    while len(cols) < K:
        ang = 2.0 * np.pi * m * t
        cols.append(np.cos(ang))
        cols.append(np.sin(ang))
        m += 1
        if len(cols) > K:
            break
    return np.column_stack(cols[:K])


def make_basis_poly(n, K):
    """Centered polynomial basis, no constant column."""
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    u = 2.0 * t - 1.0
    cols = []
    for k in range(1, K + 1):
        cols.append(u ** k)
    return np.column_stack(cols)


def profile_from_basis(theta, basis):
    """Nonnegative simplex profile from logistic-model parameters."""
    z = basis @ np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    return softmax_from_z(z)


def make_linear_profile(n, eta):
    """Monotone linear profile 1 + eta*(t-1/2)."""
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = 1.0 + eta * (t - 0.5)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    s = float(a.sum())
    return a / s


def make_cubic_profile(n, a1, a3):
    """Anti-symmetric cubic profile 1 + a1*u + a3*u^3."""
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    u = 2.0 * t - 1.0
    a = 1.0 + a1 * u + a3 * u ** 3
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    s = float(a.sum())
    return a / s


def make_deterministic_profiles(n):
    """A small set of strong analytic starting profiles."""
    profs = []
    for eta in (1.0, 1.25, 1.5, 1.75, 2.0):
        profs.append(make_linear_profile(n, eta))
    for a1, a3 in ((1.5, -1.0), (1.8, -2.0), (1.6, -1.2), (1.2, -0.5), (1.0, 1.0), (2.0, -2.0)):
        profs.append(make_cubic_profile(n, a1, a3))
    # Two additional softened monotone shapes.
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = 1.0 + 1.6 * t - 0.8 * t * t
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    profs.append(a / a.sum())
    a = 1.0 + 1.1 * t + 0.9 * t ** 3
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    profs.append(a / a.sum())
    return profs


def make_random_smooth_profiles(n, count, rng):
    """Deterministic profiles plus random smooth parameterized and power-modulated profiles."""
    profs = list(make_deterministic_profiles(n))
    bm = make_basis_mixed(n, 13)
    bp = make_basis_poly(n, 12)
    for _ in range(max(0, int(count))):
        th = rng.uniform(-3.0, 3.0, bm.shape[1])
        profs.append(profile_from_basis(th, bm))
        th = rng.uniform(-3.0, 3.0, bp.shape[1])
        profs.append(profile_from_basis(th, bp))
    extra = max(1, int(count) // 4)
    for _ in range(extra):
        th = rng.uniform(-2.5, 2.5, bm.shape[1])
        p = profile_from_basis(th, bm)
        q = np.power(p, 3.0)
        q /= q.sum()
        profs.append(q)
        p = profile_from_basis(th, bm)
        q = 0.7 * p + 0.3 * make_linear_profile(n, 1.5)
        q /= q.sum()
        profs.append(q)
    return profs


def interpolate_profile(p0, n_new):
    """Linear interpolation of a simplex profile to a new length."""
    p0 = np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n0 = p0.size
    if n0 == n_new:
        return normalize_nonneg(p0)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n0)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
    return normalize_nonneg(np.interp(x1, x0, p0))


def param_adam(theta0, basis, deadline, rng, lr=0.25, reg=1e-6):
    """
    Adam optimization in the low-dimensional parameter space theta -> softmax(basis@theta).
    Returns (best_theta, best_p, best_score).
    """
    basis = np.asarray(basis, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    dim = theta.size
    n = basis.shape[0]

    b1 = 0.90
    b2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8
    m = np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)
    adam_t = 0

    p = profile_from_basis(theta, basis)
    c = full_convolve_fft(p)
    maxc = float(c.max())
    beta = max(1e-12, 0.25 * maxc)

    best_theta = theta.copy()
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)
    no_imp = 0
    base_lr = lr

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        L, w = soft_mx(c, beta)
        gp = 2.0 * corr_weights(w, p)
        vp = gp + reg * p
        dot = float(np.dot(p, vp))
        gz = p * (vp - dot)
        gtheta = basis.T @ gz

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gtheta)):
            theta = best_theta.copy()
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            adam_t = 0
            continue

        adam_t += 1
        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * gtheta
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (gtheta * gtheta)
        mh = m / (1.0 - b1 ** adam_t)
        vh = v / (1.0 - b2 ** adam_t)
        theta = theta - lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + eps)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
            theta = best_theta.copy()
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            adam_t = 0
            continue

        theta = np.clip(theta, -6.0, 6.0)
        p = profile_from_basis(theta, basis)
        c = full_convolve_fft(p)
        maxc = float(c.max())
        score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)

        if np.isfinite(score):
            if score < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = score
                best_p = p.copy()
                best_theta = theta.copy()
                no_imp = 0
            else:
                no_imp += 1
                if no_imp % 400 == 0:
                    lr = max(0.04, lr * 0.97)
        else:
            theta = best_theta.copy()
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            adam_t = 0
            continue

        if adam_t % 100 == 0:
            beta = max(1e-12, beta * 0.75)

        if no_imp > max(250, 6 * dim):
            theta = best_theta + 0.55 * rng.normal(size=dim)
            theta = np.clip(theta, -6.0, 6.0)
            p = profile_from_basis(theta, basis)
            c = full_convolve_fft(p)
            maxc = float(c.max())
            beta = max(1e-10, 0.25 * maxc)
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            adam_t = 0
            lr = base_lr
            no_imp = 0

    return best_theta, best_p, best_score


def logit_anneal(p0, deadline, rng, lr=0.08, reg=1e-6):
    """
    Full logit-space Adam polish of a nonnegative simplex sequence.
    Returns (best_p, best_score).
    """
    p0 = np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p0 = np.maximum(p0, 1e-12)
    p0 = p0 / p0.sum()
    n = p0.size

    z = np.log(p0)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))

    b1 = 0.90
    b2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    adam_t = 0

    p = p0.copy()
    c = full_convolve_fft(p)
    maxc = float(c.max())
    beta = max(1e-12, 0.25 * maxc)

    best_p = p0.copy()
    best_z = z.copy()
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)
    no_imp = 0
    base_lr = lr

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        L, w = soft_mx(c, beta)
        gp = 2.0 * corr_weights(w, p)
        vp = gp + reg * p
        dot = float(np.dot(p, vp))
        gz = p * (vp - dot)
        gz -= float(np.mean(gz))

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gz)):
            z = best_z.copy()
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            adam_t = 0
            continue

        adam_t += 1
        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * gz
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (gz * gz)
        mh = m / (1.0 - b1 ** adam_t)
        vh = v / (1.0 - b2 ** adam_t)
        z = z - lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + eps)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
            z = best_z.copy()
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            adam_t = 0
            continue

        z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
        z -= float(np.mean(z))
        p = softmax_from_z(z)
        c = full_convolve_fft(p)
        maxc = float(c.max())
        score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)

        if np.isfinite(score):
            if score < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = score
                best_p = p.copy()
                best_z = z.copy()
                no_imp = 0
            else:
                no_imp += 1
                if no_imp % 500 == 0:
                    lr = max(0.02, lr * 0.97)
        else:
            z = best_z.copy()
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            adam_t = 0
            continue

        if adam_t % 100 == 0:
            beta = max(1e-12, beta * 0.75)

        if no_imp > max(500, 6 * n):
            z = 0.82 * best_z + 0.32 * rng.normal(size=n)
            z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            p = softmax_from_z(z)
            c = full_convolve_fft(p)
            maxc = float(c.max())
            beta = max(1e-10, 0.25 * maxc)
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            adam_t = 0
            lr = base_lr
            no_imp = 0

    return best_p, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """
    Multi-stage search for nonnegative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2.

    Strategy:
      1. Low-dimensional logistic-model gradient search (mixed trig/polynomial bases).
      2. Full logit-space Adam polish of promising profiles.
      3. Interpolation/upssaing to longer lengths and final local polish.
      4. Exact re-scoring of the best returns.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 10.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**63 - 1))

    best_seq = None
    best_score = np.inf
    candidates = []

    def local_update(p, sc):
        nonlocal best_seq, best_score
        if p is not None and sc is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_seq = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            best_score = float(sc)
        add_candidate(candidates, p, sc, 18)

    # Safe fallbacks.
    for nf, eta in ((128, 1.5), (256, 1.5), (512, 1.4)):
        p = make_linear_profile(nf, eta)
        sc = internal_score(p)
        local_update(p, sc)
        if best_score < 2.0:
            break

    # Use provided parent if valid, downsample for search if too long.
    parent_valid = np.array([])
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if parent.ndim == 1 and parent.size > 0:
            pn = normalize_nonneg(parent)
            if pn is not None:
                parent_valid = pn
    except Exception:
        parent_valid = np.array([])

    if parent_valid.size > 0:
        p_search = parent_valid if parent_valid.size <= 1024 else interpolate_profile(parent_valid, 512)
        sc_search = internal_score(p_search)
        local_update(p_search, sc_search)
        if parent_valid.size <= 4096:
            sc_parent = internal_score(parent_valid)
            local_update(parent_valid, sc_parent)

    print(f"initial best={best_score:.10f}")

    # Phase A: low-dimensional global-ish search at a modest length.
    n0 = 128
    bm0 = make_basis_mixed(n0, 13)
    bp0 = make_basis_poly(n0, 12)
    phase_a_end = min(deadline, t_start + 360.0)

    theta_starts = []
    for dims, basis in ((bm0.shape[1], bm0), (bp0.shape[1], bp0)):
        theta_starts.append((basis, dims))

    for basis, dims in theta_starts:
        starts = []
        th0 = np.zeros(dims)
        th0[0] = 1.5
        starts.append(th0)

        th1 = np.zeros(dims)
        th1[0] = 1.8
        if dims > 2:
            th1[2] = -2.0
        starts.append(th1)

        th2 = np.zeros(dims)
        th2[0] = 1.25
        if dims > 4:
            th2[4] = -1.0
        starts.append(th2)

        for _ in range(22):
            starts.append(rng.uniform(-3.0, 3.0, dims))

        for th in starts:
            if time.monotonic() > phase_a_end:
                break
            per = min(phase_a_end, time.monotonic() + 5.0)
            _, p_best, sc = param_adam(th, basis, per, rng, lr=0.25)
            local_update(p_best, sc)
            if best_score < 1.70:
                print(f"phase A low-dim best={best_score:.10f}")

    print(f"after phase A best={best_score:.10f}")

    # Phase B: full logit polish of the best low-dimensional results at n0.
    phase_b_end = min(deadline, t_start + 720.0)
    for i in range(min(10, len(candidates))):
        if time.monotonic() > phase_b_end:
            break
        p0 = candidates[i][1]
        if p0.size != n0:
            p0 = interpolate_profile(p0, n0)
        per = min(phase_b_end, time.monotonic() + 22.0)
        p_best, sc = logit_anneal(p0, per, rng, lr=0.08)
        local_update(p_best, sc)
        if best_score < 1.70:
            print(f"phase B n={n0} best={best_score:.10f}")

    print(f"after phase B best={best_score:.10f}")

    # Phase C: extend top candidates to longer lengths and polish.
    target_lengths = [256, 512]
    for tn in target_lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 160.0:
            break
        for i in range(min(6, len(candidates))):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 160.0:
                break
            p0 = interpolate_profile(candidates[i][1], tn)
            p0 = normalize_nonneg(p0)
            if p0 is None:
                continue
            if tn <= 256:
                per = min(deadline - 160.0, time.monotonic() + 26.0)
            else:
                per = min(deadline - 160.0, time.monotonic() + 38.0)
            p_best, sc = logit_anneal(p0, per, rng, lr=0.08)
            local_update(p_best, sc)
            if best_score < 1.60:
                print(f"phase C n={tn} best={best_score:.10f}")

        # A few deterministic longer profiles.
        for p0 in make_deterministic_profiles(tn)[:5]:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 160.0:
                break
            per = min(deadline - 160.0, time.monotonic() + 20.0)
            p_best, sc = logit_anneal(p0, per, rng, lr=0.08)
            local_update(p_best, sc)

    # Phase D: if time remains and score is still not excellent, directly sample longer random profiles.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0 and best_score > 1.56:
        for tn in (256, 512):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120.0:
                break
            profs = make_random_smooth_profiles(tn, 5, rng)
            for p0 in profs[:8]:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120.0:
                    break
                per = min(deadline - 120.0, time.monotonic() + (22.0 if tn <= 256 else 32.0))
                p_best, sc = logit_anneal(p0, per, rng, lr=0.08)
                local_update(p_best, sc)

    # Phase E: exact re-scoring of top internal candidates.
    if best_seq is not None:
        final_seq = best_seq
        final_score = best_score
        if final_seq.size <= 2048:
            sc = true_score(final_seq)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_score:
                final_score = sc
        # Re-evaluate top candidates exactly if safe.
        for sc_c, p_c in candidates:
            if p_c.size <= 2048:
                sc_c = true_score(p_c)
                if np.isfinite(sc_c):
                    if sc_c < final_score:
                        final_score = sc_c
                        final_seq = p_c

        # Call the authoritative evaluator once on a final candidate if length is manageable.
        if final_seq.size <= 2048:
            try:
                sc_eval = float(evaluate_sequence(final_seq.tolist()))
                if np.isfinite(sc_eval) and sc_eval < final_score:
                    final_score = sc_eval
            except Exception:
                pass

        final_seq = normalize_nonneg(final_seq)
        if final_seq is None:
            final_seq = make_linear_profile(256, 1.5)
            final_score = internal_score(final_seq)

        # Values must be positive or zero, finite, and bounded.
        final_seq = np.clip(final_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(final_seq.sum())
        if s > 0:
            final_seq = final_seq / s

        print(f"final best={final_score:.10f} n={final_seq.size}")
        return [float(x) for x in final_seq]

    # Ultimate fallback.
    fallback = make_linear_profile(256, 1.5)
    return [float(x) for x in fallback]
```
